Appendix E: Complete anihpj Cilium Configuration
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    E.1  Cilium install values for anihpj
    E.2  Complete NetworkPolicy set (frontend, backend, database)
    E.3  Hubble configuration
    E.4  WireGuard encryption config
    E.5  Prometheus ServiceMonitor for Hubble metrics
    E.6  Grafana dashboard JSON
    E.7  Canary deployment configuration

    This appendix is anihpj's single source of truth: every YAML block
    below is the SAME configuration already taught chapter-by-chapter,
    assembled in one place so you can stand up the whole thing in one
    pass. Each block cross-references the chapter that explains WHY it
    looks the way it does — treat this appendix as the "final assembly"
    instructions, not a substitute for the teaching chapters themselves.

    E.1  Cilium Install Values for anihpj
         - Assembles Ch5.3's cilium install feature flags, Ch6.4's
           supported Helm-value-then-upgrade path, and Ch14.1/Ch16.3's
           metrics + encryption flags into ONE values-anihpj.yaml — the
           file a real GitOps pipeline (ArgoCD/Helm) tracks for anihpj
         - policyEnforcementMode is left UNSET (Cilium's "default"
           factory mode, Ch7.8) — anihpj's own tier policies (E.2) each
           select their pods directly, so "default" already converges
           with "always" once applied; no override needed here

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  values-anihpj.yaml                                          │
         │                                                              │
         │  # Applied via:                                              │
         │  #   helm install cilium cilium/cilium -n kube-system \      │
         │  #     -f values-anihpj.yaml   (Ch5.2 -- Helm method,        │
         │  #                            GitOps-friendly, full control) │
         │                                                              │
         │  # --- kube-proxy replacement (Ch5.3) ---                    │
         │  kubeProxyReplacement: true                                  │
         │                                                              │
         │  # --- Hubble: CLI + Relay + UI (Ch5.3, Ch13) ---            │
         │  hubble:                                                     │
         │    enabled: true                                             │
         │    relay:                                                    │
         │      enabled: true                                           │
         │    ui:                                                       │
         │      enabled: true                                           │
         │    metrics:                                                  │
         │      enableOpenMetrics: true      # Ch6.4 worked example     │
         │      enabled:                     # Ch14.1 real metric flags │
         │        - dns                                                 │
         │        - drop                                                │
         │        - tcp                                                 │
         │        - flow                                                │
         │        - port-distribution                                   │
         │        - icmp                                                │
         │        - httpV2              # httpV2, NOT deprecated http   │
         │      serviceMonitor:                                         │
         │        enabled: true              # Ch14.2 -> generates E.5's│
         │                                    # ServiceMonitor for you  │
         │                                                              │
         │  # --- Transparent encryption (Ch5.3, Ch16.3) ---            │
         │  encryption:                                                 │
         │    enabled: true                                             │
         │    type: wireguard                                           │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  cilium install \                                            │
         │    --namespace kube-system \                                 │
         │    --set kubeProxyReplacement=true \                         │
         │    --set hubble.enabled=true \                               │
         │    --set hubble.relay.enabled=true \                         │
         │    --set hubble.ui.enabled=true \                            │
         │    --set hubble.metrics.enableOpenMetrics=true \             │
         │    --set hubble.metrics.enabled="{dns,drop,tcp,flow,\        │
         │       port-distribution,icmp,httpV2}" \                      │
         │    --set hubble.metrics.serviceMonitor.enabled=true \        │
         │    --set encryption.enabled=true \                           │
         │    --set encryption.type=wireguard                           │
         │                                                              │
         │  # Then: cilium status --wait  &&  cilium connectivity test  │
         │  # (full annotated walkthrough: Ch5.3)                       │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.2  Complete NetworkPolicy Set (Frontend, Backend, Database)
         - Every CiliumNetworkPolicy/CCNP anihpj needs, assembled from
           Ch7.3, Ch7.4, Ch8.2, Ch10.3, and Ch15.2 into one posture
         - Label convention: Ch7.3's real YAML uses component: (not
           Ch7.7's diagram-only tier:) for frontend/backend/database —
           this appendix follows the real YAML's key throughout
         - Cilium MERGES every CNP/CCNP selecting a given endpoint —
           applying several policies below to the SAME pod is additive,
           not a conflict (Ch8's Mistake 2)

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy                        │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: anihpj-default-deny-all                             │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    description: "Backstop: selects EVERY pod cluster-wide    │
         │                  with ZERO allow rules -- converges 'default'│
         │                  mode (Ch7.8) with 'always' for any pod not  │
         │                  already covered by a tier policy below"     │
         │    endpointSelector: {}                                      │
         │    ingress: []                                               │
         │    egress: []                                                │
         │                                                              │
         │  # Apply this LAST (see the Deployment Order checklist) --   │
         │  # only after each tier's own allow policy is verified, per  │
         │  # Ch7.6's audit-before-enforce rule.                        │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy                                   │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: allow-frontend-ingress                              │
         │    namespace: anihpj                                         │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    description: "Frontend (nginx) tier accepts inbound       │
         │                  HTTP/S from anywhere; everything past this  │
         │                  hits the backend tier's own policy below"   │
         │    endpointSelector:                                         │
         │      matchLabels:                                            │
         │        app: anihpj                                           │
         │        component: frontend    # nginx (Ch7.7 diagram's tier= │
         │                               # frontend, same pods)         │
         │    ingress:                                                  │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - {}                     # "{}" = ANY source (Ch7.4)    │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "80"                                          │
         │          protocol: TCP                                       │
         │        - port: "443"                                         │
         │          protocol: TCP                                       │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy                                   │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: protect-django-admin                                │
         │    namespace: anihpj                                         │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    endpointSelector:                                         │
         │      matchLabels:                                            │
         │        app: anihpj                                           │
         │    ingress:                                                  │
         │    # RULE 1: Allow GET /jobs/ from anywhere (Ch7.4)          │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - {}                                                    │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "8000"                                        │
         │        rules:                                                │
         │          http:                                               │
         │          - method: "GET"                                     │
         │            path: "/jobs/"                                    │
         │                                                              │
         │    # RULE 2: Block POST /admin/ except from internal IPs     │
         │    - fromCIDR:                                               │
         │      - "10.0.0.0/8"                                          │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "8000"                                        │
         │        rules:                                                │
         │          http:                                               │
         │          - method: "POST"                                    │
         │            path: "/admin/"                                   │
         │                                                              │
         │    # RULE 3 (extension -- completes the jobpost API surface; │
         │    # Ch7.4 illustrated only 2 rules): GET the jobpost list/  │
         │    # detail API from anywhere -- the exact path Ch15.1's 500-│
         │    # error trace and Ch15's canary comparison both observed  │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - {}                                                    │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "8000"                                        │
         │        rules:                                                │
         │          http:                                               │
         │          - method: "GET"                                     │
         │            path: "/api/jobpost/"                             │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy                                   │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: protect-postgres                                    │
         │    namespace: anihpj                                         │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    endpointSelector:                                         │
         │      matchLabels:                                            │
         │        app: postgres                                         │
         │        component: database                                   │
         │    ingress:                                                  │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - matchLabels:                                          │
         │          app: anihpj                                         │
         │          component: backend                                  │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "5432"                                        │
         │          protocol: TCP                                       │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy                                   │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: anihpj-require-mutual-auth-to-postgres              │
         │    namespace: anihpj                                         │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    endpointSelector:                                         │
         │      matchLabels:                                            │
         │        app: postgres                                         │
         │    ingress:                                                  │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - matchLabels:                                          │
         │          app: anihpj                                         │
         │        authentication:                                       │
         │          mode: "required"       # SPIFFE/SPIRE handshake,    │
         │                                 # NOT encryption (see E.4)   │
         │        toPorts:                                              │
         │        - ports:                                              │
         │          - port: "5432"                                      │
         │            protocol: TCP                                     │
         │                                                              │
         │  # Layers on TOP of protect-postgres above -- Cilium merges  │
         │  # both (Ch8 Mistake 2): the L4 allow stays, PLUS every      │
         │  # accepted connection must now also pass mTLS.              │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy                        │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: block-cloud-metadata-access                         │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    description: "Block every pod from reaching the cloud     │
         │                  instance-metadata service (SSRF / credential│
         │                  theft prevention)"                          │
         │    endpointSelector: {}                                      │
         │    egressDeny:                                               │
         │    - toCIDR:                                                 │
         │      - "169.254.169.254/32"                                  │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy                        │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: allow-prometheus-scrape-all-namespaces              │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    description: "Prometheus in monitoring/ may scrape        │
         │                  /metrics on every pod in the cluster"       │
         │    endpointSelector: {}                                      │
         │    ingress:                                                  │
         │    - fromEndpoints:                                          │
         │      - matchLabels:                                          │
         │          k8s:io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: monitoring         │
         │          app.kubernetes.io/name: prometheus                  │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "9090"                                        │
         │          protocol: TCP                                       │
         │        rules:                                                │
         │          http:                                               │
         │          - method: "GET"                                     │
         │            path: "/metrics"                                  │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumClusterwideNetworkPolicy                        │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: cluster-wide-dns-allowlist                          │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    description: "Every pod may resolve DNS via CoreDNS, but  │
         │                  may only ever RESOLVE an allow-listed set of│
         │                  external FQDNs (blocks DNS exfiltration)"   │
         │    endpointSelector: {}                                      │
         │    egress:                                                   │
         │    - toEndpoints:                                            │
         │      - matchLabels:                                          │
         │          k8s:io.kubernetes.pod.namespace: kube-system        │
         │          k8s-app: kube-dns                                   │
         │      toPorts:                                                │
         │      - ports:                                                │
         │        - port: "53"                                          │
         │          protocol: ANY                                       │
         │        rules:                                                │
         │          dns:                                                │
         │          - matchPattern: "*.anihpj.internal"                 │
         │          - matchName: "api.stripe.com"                       │
         │          - matchName: "pypi.org"                             │
         │    - toFQDNs:                                                │
         │      - matchPattern: "*.anihpj.internal"                     │
         │      - matchName: "api.stripe.com"                           │
         │      - matchName: "pypi.org"                                 │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  POLICY                           KIND   REF                 │
         │  ------------------------------   ----   ------              │
         │  anihpj-default-deny-all          CCNP   backstop            │
         │  allow-frontend-ingress           CNP    E.2                 │
         │  protect-django-admin (+rule 3)   CNP    Ch7.4               │
         │  protect-postgres                 CNP    Ch7.3               │
         │  ...mutual-auth-to-postgres       CNP    Ch10.3              │
         │  block-cloud-metadata-access      CCNP   Ch8.2               │
         │  allow-prometheus-scrape-all-ns   CCNP   Ch8.2               │
         │  cluster-wide-dns-allowlist       CCNP   Ch8.2               │
         │                                                              │
         │  Together: DEFAULT RULE deny (Ch7.7) + 3 tiers allow-        │
         │  listed + mTLS on the DB hop + cluster-wide IMDS block +     │
         │  Prometheus scrape + DNS allowlist = anihpj's full posture.  │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.3  Hubble Configuration
         - Hubble's enable flags already live in E.1's values-anihpj
           .yaml (hubble.enabled / relay.enabled / ui.enabled) -- this
           section is the OPERATIONAL half: confirming it's up, and
           how to reach it day-to-day (Ch13)

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  $ cilium status | grep -E "Hubble|ClusterMesh"              │
         │      Hubble Relay:      OK                                   │
         │      Hubble UI:         OK                                   │
         │                                                              │
         │  # If either shows nothing: Relay/UI are OPTIONAL components │
         │  # (Ch13 Mistake 1) -- confirm hubble.relay.enabled / .ui.   │
         │  # enabled actually made it into the running Helm release.   │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  # Terminal flow viewer (Ch13.3):                            │
         │  $ hubble observe --namespace anihpj --verdict DROPPED       │
         │                                                              │
         │  # Web dashboard, local port-forward (Ch13.4):               │
         │  $ cilium hubble ui                                          │
         │  # -> http://localhost:12000                                 │
         │  #    (namespace selector, top-left: pick anihpj)            │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         - Reminder (Ch13 Mistake 2): Hubble's flow data lives in an
           in-memory ring buffer, per agent -- nothing here persists
           it. That is exactly what E.5/E.6's Prometheus + Grafana
           pipeline is for; the hubble.metrics.enabled list is already
           set in E.1, and its ServiceMonitor is assembled next, in E.5.

    E.4  WireGuard Encryption Config
         - Already baked into E.1's values-anihpj.yaml (encryption.
           enabled / encryption.type: wireguard, Ch16.3) -- below is
           the standalone form for retrofitting an EXISTING anihpj
           cluster that was installed without it

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  $ helm upgrade cilium cilium/cilium -n kube-system \        │
         │      --reuse-values \                                        │
         │      --set encryption.enabled=true \                         │
         │      --set encryption.type=wireguard                         │
         │                                                              │
         │  # --reuse-values keeps every OTHER value from E.1 intact -- │
         │  # only encryption.* changes. Rolls every cilium-agent pod   │
         │  # (Ch6.4's upgrade caveat applies here too).                │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  $ cilium status | grep Encryption                           │
         │      Encryption:      OK (WireGuard)                         │
         │                                                              │
         │  $ cilium encrypt status                                     │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         - Architecture recap (Ch16.4, full diagram there): one
           WireGuard tunnel per NODE PAIR, never per pod -- every pod
           on Node A shares the SAME tunnel to Node B. Each node auto-
           generates its own keypair and publishes only the PUBLIC key
           via the CiliumNode CR's network.cilium.io/wg-pub-key
           annotation. Control-plane nodes are excluded by default
           (Ch16.4's key-rotation bootstrapping problem)
         - Encryption != mutual auth (Ch16.5): E.2's authentication:
           mode: "required" block proves WHO is connecting; this
           section makes sure the BYTES are unreadable on the wire.
           anihpj's Postgres hop (User/Author PII, Ch16.1) needs BOTH.

    E.5  Prometheus ServiceMonitor for Hubble Metrics
         - The real ServiceMonitor from Ch14.2, targeting the hubble-
           metrics Service (port 9965) that E.1's hubble.metrics.
           serviceMonitor.enabled=true generates for you

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1                        │
         │  kind: ServiceMonitor                                        │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: hubble-metrics                                      │
         │    namespace: kube-system                                    │
         │    labels:                                                   │
         │      k8s-app: hubble                                         │
         │      release: prometheus   # must match the Prometheus CR's  │
         │                            # own serviceMonitorSelector!     │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    selector:                                                 │
         │      matchLabels:                                            │
         │        k8s-app: hubble    # matches hubble-metrics Svc labels│
         │    namespaceSelector:                                        │
         │      matchNames:                                             │
         │        - kube-system        # hubble-metrics Svc lives here  │
         │    endpoints:                                                │
         │      - port: hubble-metrics    # named Service port -> 9965  │
         │        interval: 30s                                         │
         │        path: /metrics                                        │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  $ kubectl get svc hubble-metrics -n kube-system             │
         │    NAME             TYPE        PORT(S)                      │
         │    hubble-metrics   ClusterIP   9965/TCP                     │
         │                                                              │
         │  $ kubectl -n kube-system port-forward svc/hubble-metrics \  │
         │      9965:9965                                               │
         │  $ curl -s localhost:9965/metrics | grep hubble_http_requests│
         │                                                              │
         │  # In the Prometheus UI (Status > Targets): confirm hubble-  │
         │  # metrics shows State: UP -- not just "the object exists"   │
         │  # (Ch14 Mistake 2: a ServiceMonitor label mismatch with     │
         │  # Prometheus's own serviceMonitorSelector scrapes NOTHING,  │
         │  # with no error anywhere).                                  │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    E.6  Grafana Dashboard JSON
         - A representative panel skeleton, not a full production
           Grafana export -- every "expr" below is a REAL hubble_*
           PromQL query, verbatim from Ch14.3's panel-to-PromQL table

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  {                                                           │
         │    "title": "anihpj -- Cilium / Hubble Overview",            │
         │    "panels": [                                               │
         │      {                                                       │
         │        "title": "HTTP Error Rate",                           │
         │        "type": "timeseries",                                 │
         │        "targets": [ { "expr":                                │
         │          "rate(hubble_http_requests_total" +                 │
         │          "{status=~\"5..\"}[5m])" } ]                        │
         │      },                                                      │
         │      {                                                       │
         │        "title": "DNS Query Rate (by rcode)",                 │
         │        "type": "timeseries",                                 │
         │        "targets": [ { "expr":                                │
         │          "sum(rate(hubble_dns_queries_total[5m]))" +         │
         │          " by (rcode)" } ]                                   │
         │      },                                                      │
         │      {                                                       │
         │        "title": "Postgres Traffic Volume",                   │
         │        "type": "timeseries",                                 │
         │        "targets": [ { "expr":                                │
         │          "sum(rate(hubble_flows_processed_total" +           │
         │          "{destination_port=\"5432\"}[5m]))" } ]             │
         │      },                                                      │
         │      {                                                       │
         │        "title": "Drop Rate by Reason",                       │
         │        "type": "timeseries",                                 │
         │        "targets": [ { "expr":                                │
         │          "sum(rate(hubble_drop_total[5m]))" +                │
         │          " by (reason)" } ]                                  │
         │      }                                                       │
         │    ]                                                         │
         │  }                                                           │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         - Note: the " + " line joins above are this appendix's line-
           wrap only (73-char box width) -- concatenate them back into
           ONE "expr" string before pasting into a real dashboard JSON
           file. Same 4 panels, same PromQL, as Ch14.3's mapping table.

    E.7  Canary Deployment Configuration
         - The real 90/10 CiliumEnvoyConfig from Ch10.4 -- shifts 10%
           of live jobpost API traffic to anihpj-v2 with zero Ingress/
           Service changes on the client side

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  apiVersion: cilium.io/v2                                    │
         │  kind: CiliumEnvoyConfig                                     │
         │  metadata:                                                   │
         │    name: anihpj-jobpost-canary                               │
         │  spec:                                                       │
         │    services:                # the Service clients still call │
         │      - name: anihpj                                          │
         │        namespace: anihpj                                     │
         │    backendServices:         # the REAL versioned backends    │
         │      - name: anihpj-v1                                       │
         │        namespace: anihpj                                     │
         │      - name: anihpj-v2                                       │
         │        namespace: anihpj                                     │
         │    resources:               # raw Envoy xDS config, verbatim │
         │      - "@type": .../Listener                                 │
         │        name: anihpj-canary-listener                          │
         │        # ...HttpConnectionManager -> RouteConfiguration...   │
         │      - "@type": .../RouteConfiguration                       │
         │        virtual_hosts:                                        │
         │        - routes:                                             │
         │          - match: { prefix: "/" }                            │
         │            route:                                            │
         │              weighted_clusters:                              │
         │                clusters:                                     │
         │                - name: "anihpj/anihpj-v1"                    │
         │                  weight: 90   # stable, 90% of hits          │
         │                - name: "anihpj/anihpj-v2"                    │
         │                  weight: 10   # canary, 10% of hits          │
         │      # ...+ 2 Cluster (EDS) resources, one per backend...    │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

         - Watch anihpj-v2 via Hubble/Grafana (E.3/E.6) before raising
           its weight -- re-apply this SAME object with new weights,
           no redeploy needed (Ch10.4)
         - Gateway API alternative (Ch10.5): the identical 90/10 split
           expressed as a native HTTPRoute backendRefs[].weight field
           instead of hand-fed Envoy xDS -- same result, newer API,
           full YAML in Ch10.5

    [CHECKLIST: Complete Deployment Order -- E.1 through E.7]
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  1. E.1 -- Install Cilium (values-anihpj.yaml)               │
         │     Nothing else below can exist without the CRDs, the       │
         │     Agent DaemonSet, and the WireGuard datapath itself.      │
         │                                                              │
         │  2. E.3 -- Confirm Hubble Relay/UI are actually reachable    │
         │     You need working flow visibility BEFORE rolling out      │
         │     policy, so a bad selector shows up as an AUDIT verdict   │
         │     in Hubble instead of a silent, unexplained outage.       │
         │                                                              │
         │  3. E.2 -- Apply the NetworkPolicy/CCNP set, tier by tier,   │
         │     with enableAudit: true first (Ch7.6). Watch Hubble       │
         │     (step 2) for AUDIT verdicts, fix any missed allow, THEN  │
         │     drop enableAudit to enforce. Apply the cluster-wide      │
         │     default-deny-all backstop LAST, once every tier already  │
         │     has its own verified allow policy in place.              │
         │                                                              │
         │  4. E.4 -- Turn on WireGuard node-to-node encryption.        │
         │     Applied AFTER policy is stable, so any drop you see is   │
         │     unambiguously a POLICY decision, never confused with an  │
         │     encryption/tunnel problem.                               │
         │                                                              │
         │  5. E.5 -- Point Prometheus's ServiceMonitor at hubble-      │
         │     metrics:9965. Flows are now flowing correctly under      │
         │     policy + encryption -- start collecting the numbers.     │
         │                                                              │
         │  6. E.6 -- Build the Grafana dashboard on the metrics E.5    │
         │     just enabled. A dashboard is worthless before its        │
         │     underlying series exist.                                 │
         │                                                              │
         │  7. E.7 -- Roll out the canary LAST. Only shift live         │
         │     production traffic to anihpj-v2 once security (E.2),     │
         │     encryption (E.4), and observability (E.5/E.6) are        │
         │     already in place to catch a bad canary immediately.      │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Appendix F: CCA Exam Day Checklist
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    This appendix is a literal, printable EXAM-DAY-LOGISTICS checklist — a
    companion to Chapter 19's exam STRATEGY and Chapter 20's practice EXAM,
    not a repeat of either one. Every fact reused below (90 minutes, online-
    proctored, 8 official domains, the 20/18/16/10/10/10/10/6 weight split,
    the ~90-second flag-and-skip rule) is carried over UNCHANGED from
    Chapter 19 — print this page and check items off by hand as you go.

    F.1  Week before: review domains, practice exam
         - Review all 8 official CCA domains, weighted by their REAL exam
           percentage, not an even split — same weights as the front-matter
           OFFICIAL CCA BLUEPRINT panel and the Ch19.1 time-allocation table
         - Take the FULL Ch20 70-question practice exam in one 90-minute
           sitting, then score yourself with Ch20's domain-by-domain
           scoring guide
         - Identify your weakest domain(s) from that real score — not a
           guess about which domain "felt" hard
         - Re-study ONLY the domain(s) that actually scored weak

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  F.1 WEEK-BEFORE CHECKLIST — 8 Domains, Weighted Study       │
         │                                                              │
         │  ☐ Domain 1  Architecture (20%)              — Ch1, Ch4      │
         │  ☐ Domain 2  Network Policy (18%)            — Ch7, Ch8      │
         │  ☐ Domain 3  Service Mesh (16%)               — Ch10, Ch16   │
         │  ☐ Domain 4  Observability (10%)                  — Ch13     │
         │  ☐ Domain 5  Installation & Config (10%)      — Ch5, Ch6     │
         │  ☐ Domain 6  Cluster Mesh (10%)                   — Ch17     │
         │  ☐ Domain 7  eBPF (10%)                       — Ch3, Ch11    │
         │  ☐ Domain 8  BGP & External Networking (6%)  — Ch12, Ch18    │
         │                                                              │
         │  ☐ Take the FULL Ch20 70-Q practice exam, one 90-min sitting │
         │  ☐ Score yourself domain-by-domain (Ch20's scoring guide)    │
         │  ☐ Identify your 1-2 WEAKEST domains from that real score    │
         │  ☐ Re-study ONLY those weak domain(s) — not everything again │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.2  Day before: light review, set up workspace
         - Light review ONLY — re-read your own notes/summaries; do not
           start new chapters or cram new material the day before
         - Re-open the Ch19.2 documentation-bookmarks list and click every
           single bookmarked URL to confirm it still resolves
         - Physically prep the exam space, then get a full night's sleep —
           no late cramming session

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  F.2 DAY-BEFORE CHECKLIST — Light Review, No New Material    │
         │                                                              │
         │  ☐ Re-read your OWN notes/summaries only — no new chapters   │
         │  ☐ Skim the 19.3 Top-10-Mistakes table's FIX column once     │
         │  ☐ Re-open the 19.2 documentation-bookmarks list             │
         │  ☐ Click EVERY bookmarked docs.cilium.io URL — confirm it    │
         │    still resolves (pages move between Cilium doc versions)   │
         │  ☐ Re-bookmark or fix any broken/redirected link found above │
         │  ☐ Prep the physical desk: charger, water, ID, quiet room    │
         │  ☐ Clear the desk of extra monitors/papers/phones (proctor   │
         │    rules — confirm your provider's exact desk policy today)  │
         │  ☐ Set TWO alarms and confirm the exam start time + timezone │
         │  ☐ Get a FULL night's sleep — no late cramming session       │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.3  Exam day: system check, ID ready, quiet room
         - Re-run yesterday's webcam/ID/room system check as a final
           CONFIRMATION, not the first time you're running it — this is
           exactly the failure mode described in Ch19's Mistake #2
         - Valid, unexpired photo ID staged; room confirmed quiet and
           private for the full 90 minutes
         - Water and a bathroom break BEFORE check-in — the room is closed
           once the online-proctored session begins
         - Restart the computer fresh to clear background apps/processes

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  F.3 EXAM-DAY CHECKLIST — Final Confirm, Not First Test      │
         │                                                              │
         │  ☐ Re-run the proctoring software's system/webcam check —    │
         │    this CONFIRMS yesterday's test still passes, it is not    │
         │    the first time you are running it (per Ch19 Mistake #2)   │
         │  ☐ Valid, unexpired photo ID staged next to the keyboard     │
         │  ☐ Room re-confirmed quiet, private, and free of other       │
         │    people walking through for the full 90 minutes            │
         │  ☐ Desk cleared again — no extra monitors, notes, or phones  │
         │  ☐ Bathroom break taken and water poured BEFORE check-in     │
         │    (closed-room/no-leaving rules start once the exam does)   │
         │  ☐ Computer restarted fresh to clear background apps/        │
         │    notifications that could interrupt proctoring focus       │
         │  ☐ docs.cilium.io bookmarks bar open and ready in the        │
         │    allowed browser tab before the clock starts               │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    F.4  During exam: time management, bookmark strategy
         - Stuck on a question? Flag it and move on after ~90 seconds —
           unchanged from Ch19.3's Mistake #10 fix
         - Keep the Ch19.1 domain time-budget table open as a live pacing
           reference (Architecture 18m, Network Policy 16m, Service Mesh
           14m, then 9m each for Observability/Install/Cluster Mesh/eBPF,
           6m for BGP)
         - Use the open-book docs.cilium.io access to SEARCH a specific
           field/flag/CRD name, not to browse or learn a concept cold
         - In the final minutes, revisit ONLY the questions you flagged

         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  F.4 DURING-EXAM CHECKLIST — Pacing & Open-Book Strategy     │
         │                                                              │
         │  ☐ Stuck on a question? Flag it and skip after ~90 seconds   │
         │    — same rule as Ch19.3 Mistake #10, no exceptions today    │
         │  ☐ Keep the 19.1 time-allocation table in mind as you go:    │
         │    Architecture 18m, Net Policy 16m, Svc Mesh 14m, then      │
         │    9m each for Observability/Install/ClusterMesh/eBPF,       │
         │    6m for BGP — 90 min total across the 8 domains            │
         │  ☐ Use docs.cilium.io only to SEARCH a specific field/flag   │
         │    name — never to browse or learn a concept cold, live      │
         │  ☐ After the last question, return to every flagged item     │
         │  ☐ Spend the final minutes ONLY on flagged questions —       │
         │    do not reopen answered questions you already trust        │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    🖨️  Printable Master Checklist — Morning-Of, At A Glance
         The four checklists above are for preparation over the week
         leading up to the exam. This last one combines their single
         highest-priority item each into ONE page — print just this box
         and keep it on the desk the morning of the exam.
